\,\mskip 0.99998mu|\mskip 0.99998muq_{i+1}/q_{i}|<1\,
for all

\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
, with a branch of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 2.00002muq_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu
fixed for each

i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N
, and holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
in
\,L\,
.
Both spaces
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
and
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{{\lower 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{v}$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 2.00002mu
are subspaces of
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}
,
see Proposition
4.16
and Theorem
4.21
.
Let

(4.62)

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\mskip-2.00002muL}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}},\qquad v\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv\,,\ignorespaces

be the
\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\mskip 0.99998mu
-linear extension of the map
\,\mu^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{{\lower 1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{v}$}}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
.

Recall the functions

C_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\mskip 2.00002mue^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,(n-\lambda_{i})\sum_{a=\smash{\lambda^{\mskip-2.00002mu(i-1)}}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}^{\lambda_{i}}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

and

G_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\prod_{b=\lambda^{(i)}+1}^{\lambda^{(i+1)}}\!\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\,,\kern-5.0pt\ignorespaces

see (
4.51
), (
4.52
). Define a map

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\,,\kern-6.00006pt

(4.63)

\displaystyle[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\,\mapsto\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,C_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,G_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};h;\kappa)\,\frac{W_{I}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}{c_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}\;v_{I}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

where
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\in{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
stands for the class of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\acute{h}\vphantom{h})\,
. By Proposition
4.22
, the map

\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
sends the class
\,Y\!\in{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
to the principal term
of the solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi_{Y}\mskip 0.99998mu
of the joint system of dynamical differential
equations (
2.7
) and
qKZ
difference equations (
2.5
).

Proposition 4.25
.

The map
\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\mskip 0.99998mu

is well-defined and the following diagram is commutative,

(4.64)

\textstyle{{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\;\;\,
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}

\;\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\mskip-2.00002muL}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
\textstyle{\!\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}\mskip 0.99998mu}

Proof.

By Lemma
4.4
, poles of the sum in the right-hand side of (
4.63
)
are at most those of the function
\,G_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,
and, therefore,
in addition to hyperplanes (
4.61
) can occur only at the hyperplanes

\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu=z_{b}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
\,a\neq b
. However, the sums

\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\acute{P}\vphantom{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,C_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,G_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};h;\kappa)\,\frac{W_{I}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}{c_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}\ignorespaces

are regular at the hyperplanes
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu=z_{b}\mskip 2.00002mu
for all
\,a,b\,
,
by the standard reasoning. Hence, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is well-defined.

The commutativity of diagram (
4.64
) follows from
Proposition
4.22
.
∎

Remark 4.26
.

Since
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
in
\,L\,
,
and
\,\bigl(\det\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu,

is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
, see (
4.49
), the inverse matrix

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
in
\,L\,
.
Therefore, for every
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi\in\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}
, its principal term

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}}}\mskip-0.99998mu=\widehat{\Psi}^{-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi\,
, defined by (
4.50
),
belongs to
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\,
, and there is an isomorphism

\,\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}\mskip 0.99998mu\!\to({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\,
,
\;\Psi\mapsto\Psi^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
.
The inverse map equals
\,\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\mskip-2.00002muL}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
, see (
4.62
), so that,

\,\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}\mskip-2.00002mu=\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{{\lower 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{v}$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\,
.

4.10.
Example
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,

Throughout this section, let
\,N=2\,
and
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,
.
Denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]
the element

\bigl(\{a\},\{\mskip 0.99998mu1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1,a+1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\mskip 0.99998mu\}\bigr)\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}
.
The space
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
has a basis
\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mu1\mskip 0.99998mu]},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak v_{[\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu
,
where
\,v_{[a]}\mskip-0.99998mu=v_{2}^{\otimes(a-1)}\mskip-0.99998mu\otimes v_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu\otimes v_{2}^{\otimes(n-a)}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
Clearly
\,e_{1,1}^{(a)}v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}=\mskip 0.99998mu\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,
and

\,e_{2,2}^{(a)}v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}=(1-\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub})\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,
.

The
qKZ
operators
\,K_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak K_{n}\mskip 2.00002mu
, see (
2.3
), are

(4.65)

\displaystyle\kern 11.99998ptK_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,={}

\displaystyle\,R^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mua-1)}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a-1}+\kappa;h)\,\dots\,R^{(a,1)}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{1}+\kappa;h)\mskip 2.00002mu\times{}

\displaystyle\mskip-2.00002mu{}\times\mskip-0.99998mu\;q_{1}^{e_{1,1}^{(a)}}\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}^{e_{2,2}^{(a)}}\,R^{(a,n)}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{n}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,\dots\,R^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mua+1)}(z_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{i+1}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,.

The
\mskip 0.99998muR\mskip 0.99998mu
-matrices in the right-hand side preserve the subspace
({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\subset\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}
, acting there as follows,

\displaystyle R^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}(z\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,v_{[a]}\,=\,\frac{z}{z-h}\,v_{[a]}\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{h}{z-h}\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\kern 16.00008ptR^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}(z\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,=\,\frac{z}{z-h}\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{h}{z-h}\,v_{[a]}\,,\kern-1.99997pt

\displaystyle R^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}(z\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,v_{[c]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002muv_{[c]}\,,\qquad c\neq a,b\,.\kern-10.00002pt

The
qKZ
difference equations (
2.5
) are

(4.66)

f(z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a}+\kappa,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n};h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,K_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\mskip 2.00002mu.\kern-10.00002pt

The dynamical Hamiltonians
\,X_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muX_{2}\mskip 2.00002mu
, see (
2.8
),
act on
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
as follows

(4.67)

\displaystyle X_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,z_{a}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip 2.00002muh\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{b=1}^{a-1}\mskip 0.99998muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip 2.00002mu\frac{h\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}}{q_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu-q_{2}}\,\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b=1}{\scriptstyle b\ne a}}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu,

\displaystyle X_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 2.00002muX_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 2.00002mu+\sum_{b=1}^{n}\,z_{b}\Bigr)\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,

and the dynamical differential equations (
2.7
) are

(4.68)

\displaystyle\kappa\mskip 2.00002muq_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial}{\partial\mskip 0.99998muq_{1}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002muX_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle\kappa\mskip 2.00002muq_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial}{\partial\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002muX_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-10.00002pt

In this section, we use the variable
\,t=t^{(1)}_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu
.
The substitution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{t}}=\Sigma_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}\mskip 2.00002mu
reads as
\,t\mskip 0.99998mu=z_{a}\,
.
The weight functions are

(4.69)

W_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,=\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,(t-z_{b})\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu(t-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-h)\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

We have
\,c_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;h)=1\,
, see (
4.5
).
The permutations
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{[1]},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\sigma_{[n]}\,
are

\sigma_{[a]}(1)=a\,,\qquad\sigma_{[a]}(b)=b-1\,,\quad b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1\,,\qquad\sigma_{[a]}(b)=b\,,\quad b=a+1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\ignorespaces

and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu|\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{[a]}\mskip 0.99998mu|=a-1\,
. We have

(4.70)

\displaystyle W_{[a]}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,=\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-h)\,,

\displaystyle W_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu0\,,\qquad b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1\,,

and
\;W_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,
is divisible by the product

\;\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-h)\,
for any
\,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
,
cf. Lemmas
4.3
,
4.4

The functions
\,{\check{W}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,
, see (
4.7
), are

(4.71)

{\check{W}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,=\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,(t-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-h)\prod_{c=a+1}^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu(t-z_{b})\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Set

\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b=1}{\scriptstyle b\ne a}}^{n}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})\,,\qquad\bar{Q}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,=\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b=1}{\scriptstyle b\ne a}}^{n}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-h)\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-10.00002pt

Then
\,\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})=R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{[a]}})\,,
and

\,\bar{Q}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)=Q_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{[a]}};h)\,
, where the functions

\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\,Q_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,
, are given by (
4.8
).
Biorthogonality relations (
4.9
) , (
4.10
) become

\displaystyle\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,\frac{W_{[a]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,{\check{W}}_{[b]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}{\bar{R}_{c}({\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu\bar{Q}_{c}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}\,=\,\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,,\kern-18.00005pt

\displaystyle\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,W_{[c]}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,{\check{W}}_{[c]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,=\,\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu\bar{Q}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,.\kern-10.00002pt

The master function, see (
4.11
), is

\displaystyle\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,={}

\displaystyle\!\mskip-0.99998mu{}=\,(e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}}q_{2})^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,(e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,(n-2)}q_{1}/q_{2})^{\mskip 0.99998mut/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(t-z_{a})/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-t+h)/\kappa\bigr)\,.\kern-8.99994pt

The
hypergeometric solutions (
4.20
) of the joint system of
differential equations (
4.68
) and
difference equations (
4.66
) have the form

\Psi_{[a]}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\frac{(1-q_{2}/q_{1})^{h\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}}{\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma(h/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa)}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\,\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\;W_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\ignorespaces

where the residues of the master function are

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\,q_{1}^{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{c=1,\mskip 2.00002muc\neq a}^{n}\mskip-0.99998muz_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne a}}^{n}\,\frac{\pi\mskip 2.00002mue^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}+\mskip 0.99998muz_{c})/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}}{\sin\mskip 0.99998mu\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-2.00002mu-z_{c})/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\bigr)}\,\times{}

\displaystyle{}\mskip 2.00002mu\times\,\frac{\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu(q_{2}/q_{1})^{\mskip 0.99998mul}\,\Gamma(l+h/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa)}{l\mskip 0.99998mu!}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne a}}^{n}\,\frac{\Gamma\bigl(l+(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)}{\Gamma\bigl(l+1+(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a})/\kappa\bigr)}\;.

Determinant formula for coordinates of the
hypergeometric solutions, see (

), is

(4.72)

\displaystyle\det\mskip 0.99998mu\biggl(\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}

\displaystyle{}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,W_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\mskip-2.00002mu\biggr)_{\!\mskip-2.00002mua,\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu=1}^{\!\mskip-0.99998mun}\mskip-0.99998mu={}

\displaystyle{}=\,\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\mskip-0.99998mu\bigl(\mskip 0.99998muq_{1}\mskip 2.00002muq_{2}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\mskip 2.00002mu\bigl(\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma(h/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa)\bigr)^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu}{(1-q_{2}/q_{1})^{n\mskip 0.99998muh\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}}\;

\displaystyle\prod_{a=1}^{n-1}\,\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\!\frac{\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{2}\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma\bigl(1+(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)}{e^{-\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}+\mskip 0.99998muz_{b})/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu\sin\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)}\;.\kern-3.99994pt

), (

\displaystyle\det\mskip 0.99998mu\biggl(\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{c}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mut^{\mskip 0.99998mua-1}\mskip 0.99998mue^{-\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\mskip 0.99998mu(b-1)\mskip 2.00002mut/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)\mskip-2.00002mu\biggr)_{\!\mskip-2.00002mua,\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu=1}^{\!\mskip-0.99998mun}\mskip-0.99998mu={}\hphantom{=\sum\mskip 0.99998mu}

\displaystyle{}=\,\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\mskip-0.99998mu\bigl(\mskip 0.99998muq_{1}\mskip 2.00002muq_{2}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\mskip 2.00002mu\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip-0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n-1)/2}\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n+1)/2}\mskip 2.00002mu\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\Gamma(h/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa)\bigr)^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu}{(1-q_{2}/q_{1})^{n\mskip 0.99998muh\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}}\,\times{}

\displaystyle{}\times\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{a=1}^{n-1}\,\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\!\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle\mskip 0.99998mu.

Let
\,\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}=\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{1,1}\,
.
For a Laurent polynomial
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\acute{h}\vphantom{h})\,
, we have

\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,=\,P(e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\gamma\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 0.99998mue^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{n}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 0.99998mue^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,h\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa})\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

The solution
\,\Psi_{P}\mskip 2.00002mu
of differential equations (
4.68
) and difference
equations (
4.66
) corresponding to
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\acute{h}\vphantom{h})\,
is

(4.74)

\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,\Psi_{[a]}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-11.99998pt\ignorespaces

By Proposition
4.16
,
\,\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h,{\boldsymbol{q}}\,

provided
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h\not\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\leqslant 0}\,
for all
\,a,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
,

\,a\neq b\,
, and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu|\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}/q_{1}|<1\,
with branches of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998muq_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu
and

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu
fixed. The singularities of
\,\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu

at the hyperplanes
\;z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\leqslant 0}\,
are simple poles.

The solution
\,\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
can be written as an integral
over a suitable contour
\,C\mskip 2.00002mu
encircling the poles of the product

\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998mu\Gamma\bigl({(t-z_{a})/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\bigr)\,
counterclockwise
and separating them from the poles of the product

\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-t+h)/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\bigr)\,
,

(4.75)

\displaystyle\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,={}

\displaystyle\!{}=\,\frac{(1-q_{2}/q_{1})^{h\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}}{2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu\Gamma(h/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa)}\;\int\limits_{\!\!C\,}\mskip-0.99998mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,v_{[a]}\,d\mskip 0.99998mut\,.

For instance, if
\,h/\kappa\,
is sufficiently large positive real,
the integral can be taken over the parabola

Formula (

) can be used to give an alternative proof of
analytic properties of the function
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
.

Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}\mskip-2.00002mu,\acute{h}\vphantom{h}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 0.99998mu

For

can be presented as follows

(4.76)

The algebra

is the equivariant

where the
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\operatorname{End}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigl(({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\bigr)
-valued function

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\#\mskip-0.99998mu$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\,
is as follows,

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\#\mskip-0.99998mu$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\#\mskip-0.99998mu$}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\,v_{[a]}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\#\mskip-0.99998mu$}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})\,={}\\
&{}\!\mskip-2.00002mu=\,(1-q_{2}/q_{1})^{h}\,\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,(q_{2}/q_{1})^{l}\,\sum_{c=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002mu\frac{W_{[a]}(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,{\check{W}}_{[b]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}{\bar{R}_{c}({\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu\bar{Q}_{c}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)}\,\prod_{d=1}^{n}\,\prod_{m=0}^{l-1}\,\frac{z_{d}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu+h+m}{z_{d}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu+1+m}\;.\kern-13.99995pt\end{aligned}

Furthermore, one has

and

For a solution

\Psi^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,=\,\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\,\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\ignorespaces

The principal term of the solution
\,\Psi_{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
,
corresponding to a Laurent polynomial
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\acute{h}\vphantom{h})\,
,
see (
4.53
), equals

(4.77)

\displaystyle\Psi^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[a]}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\;e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\left(\mskip-0.99998mu(n-2)\mskip 0.99998muz_{b}\mskip 0.99998mu+\sum_{c=1}^{n}z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)}\mskip 0.99998mu\times{}

\displaystyle{}\times\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne b}}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c})/\kappa\bigr)\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle\mskip 0.99998mu.

Let
\,L\,
be the complement of the union of the hyperplanes

z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\neq 0}\,,\qquad z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\leqslant 0}\,,\qquad a,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\quad a\neq b\,,\kern-6.00006pt\ignorespaces

cf. (
4.61
). Denote by
\,{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\mskip 0.99998mu
the ring of
functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
holomorphic in
\,L\,
. The map

(4.78)

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} {[P\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,\mapsto\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[a]}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\;e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\left.\left(\mskip-0.99998mu(n-2)\mskip 0.99998muz_{b}\mskip 0.99998mu+\sum_{c=1}^{n}z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)\mskip-0.99998mu\right/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu\times{}&\kern-10.00002pt\\[-6.0pt]
{}\times\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne b}}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c})/\kappa\bigr)\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu+h)/\kappa\bigr)\mskip 2.00002mu&\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-10.00002pt\end{aligned}

sends the class
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\in{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\acute{h}\vphantom{h})\,
to the principal term of the solution

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi_{P}\mskip 0.99998mu
of the joint system of differential equations (
4.68
)
and difference equations (
4.66
).

Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}
be the space
of
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
-valued solutions of dynamical differential equations (
4.68
)
holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{q}}\,
provided
\,\mskip 0.99998mu|\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}/q_{1}|<1\,
with branches of

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998muq_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu
and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998muq_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu
fixed, and holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,

in
\,L\,
. The space
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is a subspace of
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}
.

Since the matrix
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,

in
\,L\,
, and
\,\bigl(\det\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is entire
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
, the inverse matrix
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is also
holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},h\,
in
\,L\,
. Thus the map

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\mskip-2.00002muL}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}},\qquad v\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\widehat{\Psi}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv\,,\ignorespaces

gives an isomorphism of
\,{\mathcal{O}}_{L}\mskip 0.99998mu
-modules.
Furthermore, the following diagram is commutative,

\textstyle{{\mathcal{K}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\;\;\,
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}_{L}

\;\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\mskip-2.00002muL}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
\textstyle{\!\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muL}}\mskip 0.99998mu}

see Proposition
4.25
.

5.
Limit
\,{h\to\infty}\,
for solutions of the dynamical and
qKZ

equations

5.1.
Limiting weight functions
W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})

For
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, define the
limiting weight functions

(5.1)

\displaystyle W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\operatorname{Sym}_{\mskip 2.00002mut^{(1)}_{1}\!,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\,t^{(1)}_{\lambda^{(1)}}}\,\ldots\;\operatorname{Sym}_{\mskip 2.00002mut^{(N-1)}_{1}\!,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\,t^{(N-1)}_{\lambda^{(N-1)}}}U^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{I}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,,

\displaystyle U^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{I}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{j=1}^{N-1}\,\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(j)}}\,\biggl(\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle i^{(j+1)}_c\?<\>i^{(j)}_a}}^{\lambda^{(j+1)}}\!\!(t^{(j)}_{a}\mskip-2.00002mu-t^{(j+1)}_{c})\,\prod_{b=a+1}^{\lambda^{(j)}}\frac{1}{t^{(j)}_{b}\mskip-2.00002mu-t^{(j)}_{a}}\,\biggr)\,.

Recall the element
I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
and the permutation
\,\sigma_{I}\,
. Set

d_{I}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{j=1}^{N-1}\lambda^{(j)}(\lambda^{(j+1)}-1)-|\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu|\,.

Lemma 5.1
.

For
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, we have

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\lim_{h\to\infty}(-h)^{-d_{I}}\,W_{I}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,.

Proof.

The statement follows from formulae (
4.2
), (
5.1
) by induction
on the length of
\sigma_{I}\,
.
∎

Example
.

Let
N=2
,
n=2
,
{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,1)
,
I=(\{1\},\{2\})
,
J=(\{2\},\{1\})
. Then

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,1\,,\qquad W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muJ}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,t^{(1)}_{1}\mskip-2.00002mu-z_{1}\,.\ignorespaces

Lemma 5.2
.

We have
\;W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})=1\,
and

(5.2)

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\sigma_{0}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{j=1}^{N-1}\,\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(j)}}\,\prod_{b=\smash{\lambda^{(j)}}+1}^{\lambda^{(j+1)}}\!(t_{a}^{(j)}\mskip-2.00002mu-z_{n-b+1})\,,\ignorespaces

where
\,\sigma_{0}\mskip-0.99998mu\in S_{n}\mskip 2.00002mu
is the longest permutation,

\,\sigma_{0}(a)=n+1-a\,
,
\;a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
.

Let
\;\Upsilon_{\!{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}=\{\,i\ |\ \,\lambda_{i+1}\mskip-0.99998mu\neq 0\,\}\subset\{\mskip 0.99998mu1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\mskip 0.99998mu\}\,
.

Lemma 5.3
.

For the transpositions
\;s_{\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-2.00002mu,\,\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}\,,\,\;i\in\Upsilon_{\!{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
,
we have

(5.3)

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002mus_{\lambda^{(i)}\!,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\sum_{j=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\;(\mskip 0.99998mut^{(i)}_{j}\mskip-2.00002mu-z_{j})\,.

Lemmas
5.2
and
5.3
are proved in Appendix
C

The following lemma describes the
\,h\to\infty\,
limit of the three-term
relations of Lemma
4.1
for functions
\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)\,
.

Lemma 5.4
.

For any
\,I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
,
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
, and
\,a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n-1\,
, we have

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{s_{a\mskip-0.99998mu,a+1}(I)}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\frac{W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})-W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a+1},z_{a},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n})}{z_{a+1}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a}}\;,\ignorespaces

if
\,|\mskip 0.99998mus_{a,\mskip 0.99998mua+1}\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu|<|\sigma_{I}|\,
, and

\;W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a+1},z_{a},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n})\,=\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, otherwise.

Proof.

Notice that
\,\sigma_{s_{a\mskip-0.99998mu,a+1}(I)}=\mskip 0.99998mus_{a,\mskip 0.99998mua+1}\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{I}\,
unless

\,s_{a,\mskip 0.99998mua+1}(I)=I\,
. Now the statement follows from Lemmas
5.1

and
4.1
.
∎

Remark 5.5
.

Define the operators
\Delta_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\Delta_{n-1}
acting on functions of
\,z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n}\,
:

\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}\mskip 0.99998muf({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\frac{f({\boldsymbol{z}})-f(z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a+1},z_{a},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n})}{z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a+1}}\;.\kern-16.00008pt

They satisfy the nil-Coxeter relations,

\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}^{2}=\mskip 0.99998mu0\,,\kern 18.00005pt\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mub}=\mskip 0.99998mu\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mub}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}\,,\quad|\mskip 0.99998mua-b\mskip 0.99998mu|>1\,,\kern 18.00005pt\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{a+1}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}=\mskip 0.99998mu\Delta_{a+1}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{\mskip 0.99998mua}\mskip 2.00002mu\Delta_{a+1}\,,\kern-40.00006pt\ignorespaces

for any
\,a,b\,
.

Denote
\,{\boldsymbol{y}}=(y_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak y_{n})\,
. Let the functions
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widetilde{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu\!I}({\boldsymbol{y}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,

be obtained from
\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
by the substitution

\,t^{(i)}_{j}\!=y_{j}\,
,
\;i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
,
\,j=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\lambda^{(i)}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
By Lemmas
5.2
,
5.4
,
B.4
, and Proposition
B.3
,
the functions
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widetilde{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu\!I}({\boldsymbol{y}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
coincide with the
A\mskip 0.99998mu
-type double
Schubert polynomials
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma}({\boldsymbol{y}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,

(5.4)

\mskip 0.99998mu\widetilde{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu\!I}({\boldsymbol{y}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{y}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,.\kern-10.00002pt

For
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, define

(5.5)

{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu\sigma_{0}(I)}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;z_{n},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{1})\,,\ignorespaces

where
\,\sigma_{0}\,
is the longest permutation. Recall
\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
see (
4.8
), and
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma}\mskip-0.99998mu=(z_{\sigma(1)},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{\sigma(n)})\,
.

Lemmas
5.6
and
5.7
below follow by Lemma
5.1
from
Lemma
4.3
, Proposition
4.5
, and Corollary
4.6
,
respectively. Lemmas
5.6
and
5.7
are equivalent to the
vanishing and orthogonality properties of the double Schubert polynomials.

Lemma 5.6
.

For
\,I,J\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, we have
\;W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\Sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h)=0\mskip 2.00002mu
unless
\,I=J
or

\,|\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu|>|\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu|\,
, and

(5.6)

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{j=1}^{N-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{k=j+1}^{N}\,\prod_{a\in I_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b\in I_k}{\scriptstyle b<a}}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})\,.\kern-13.99995pt

Lemma 5.7
.

The functions
\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu
and
\;{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muJ}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu

are biorthogonal,

(5.7)

\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\,\frac{W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\Sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muK}(\Sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\,=\,\delta_{J,\mskip 0.99998muK}\,,\kern 18.00005pt\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!I}(\Sigma_{K};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\delta_{J,\mskip 0.99998muK}\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)\,.\kern-3.99994pt\ignorespaces

5.2.
Limiting master function

Define the
limiting master function
:

(5.8)

\displaystyle\kern 3.99994pt\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\,(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{N}\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mun}\mskip 0.99998mup_{N})^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\,\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}+\lambda_{i+1}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}/p_{i+1}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\mskip 0.99998mut^{(i)}_{a}\!\mskip-0.99998mu/\kappa}\times{}

\displaystyle{}\times\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\biggl(\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b=1}{\scriptstyle b\ne a}}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\frac{1}{\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(t_{a}^{(i)}\mskip-2.00002mu-t_{b}^{(i)})/\kappa\bigr)}\,\prod_{c=1}^{\lambda^{(i+1)}}\mskip-0.99998mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(t_{a}^{(i)}-t_{c}^{(i+1)})/\kappa\bigr)\mskip-0.99998mu\biggr)\,,

where
\,\lambda^{(N)}\mskip-2.00002mu=n\,
and
\,t^{(N)}_{a}\mskip-2.00002mu=z_{a}\,
,
\;a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
.
The last formula can be also written as

\displaystyle\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\,(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{N}\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mun}\mskip 0.99998mup_{N})^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\,\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}+\lambda_{i+1}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}/p_{i+1}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\mskip 0.99998mut^{(i)}_{a}\!\mskip-0.99998mu/\kappa}\times{}

\displaystyle{}\times\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\biggl(\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,\frac{(t_{a}^{(i)}\mskip-2.00002mu-t_{b}^{(i)})\mskip 0.99998mu\sin\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(t_{b}^{(i)}\mskip-2.00002mu-t_{a}^{(i)})/\kappa\bigr)}{\pi\kappa}\,\prod_{c=1}^{\lambda^{(i+1)}}\mskip-0.99998mu\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(t_{a}^{(i)}-t_{c}^{(i+1)})/\kappa\bigr)\mskip-0.99998mu\biggr)\,,

Denote by
\,\widetilde{\Phi}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
the function obtained from
the master function
\,\Phi_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
by the substitution

(5.9)

q_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,p_{i}\,h^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}}\,e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,(\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mun)}\mskip 2.00002mu,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

cf. (
3.9
).

Recall
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}\mskip-0.99998mu=\sum_{i=1}^{N-1}\lambda^{(i)}
,

\,\lambda^{\{2\}}=\,\sum_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\lambda^{(i)}\bigr)^{2}
,

\,\lambda_{\{2\}}=\sum_{1\leqslant i<j\leqslant N}\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{j}\,
.

Lemma 5.8
.

For
\,|\mskip-0.99998mu\arg\mskip 0.99998mu(h/\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu|<\pi
,  we have

\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\lim_{h\to\infty}\,(\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998muh)^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{2\}}\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}}\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\Gamma(h/\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{\{2\}}}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widetilde{\Phi}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-16.00008pt\ignorespaces

Proof.

The statement follows from formulae (
4.11
), (
5.8
)
by Stirling’s formula

(5.10)

\frac{\Gamma(\alpha+h/\kappa)}{\Gamma(\beta+h/\kappa)}\;\sim\,(h/\kappa)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\alpha-\beta}\mskip 2.00002mu,\qquad h\to\infty\,,\quad|\mskip-0.99998mu\arg\mskip 0.99998mu(h/\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu|<\pi\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

∎

5.3.
Solutions of the limiting dynamical and

equations

For a polynomial
\,f({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, define the Jackson integral

(5.11)

{\mathcal{M}}_{J}(\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}f)({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\!\sum_{{\boldsymbol{l}}\in{\mathbb{Z}}^{\lambda^{\{1\}}}\!\!\!\!}\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}-\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu}\bigl(\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muf({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr)\,,\qquad J\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,.\kern-13.00005pt\ignorespaces

Notice that the master function
\,\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

has only simple poles, and

\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}-\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu}\bigl(\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muf({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr)\,=\,f(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-0.99998mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}-\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

A closed expression for the residue

\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}-\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

is given by Lemma
5.9
below.

Set

(5.12)

M^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\pi^{-\lambda_{\{2\}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\prod_{b=\lambda^{(i)}+1}^{\lambda^{(i+1)}}\!\sin\mskip 0.99998mu\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-2.00002mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

cf. (

Lemma 5.9
.

If
\;{\boldsymbol{l}}\not\in\mskip-0.99998mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{\lambda^{\{1\}}}\mskip-2.00002mu
, then

.
For
\;{\boldsymbol{l}}\in\mskip-0.99998mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{\lambda^{\{1\}}}\mskip-2.00002mu
,

(5.14)

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}-\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\frac{A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-0.99998mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)}{M^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)}\,\times{}

\displaystyle{}\,\times\,\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a\in J_{i}}\mskip-2.00002muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(-\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa)^{-\lambda_{i}-\lambda_{i+1}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i+1}/p_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}l_{a}^{(i)}}\!.

In particular,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Sigma_{J}}\mskip-2.00002mu\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,={}

\displaystyle{}=\,\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a\in J_{i}}\mskip-2.00002muz_{a}/\kappa}\;\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{N}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{a\in J_{i}}\,\prod_{b\in J_{j}}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)\,.\kern-0.59998pt

5.9
, the actual summation in formula (

)

Lemma 5.10
.

\,|\mskip-0.99998mu\arg\mskip 0.99998mu(h/\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu|<\pi
,  we have

Define

\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!J}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\kappa^{-\lambda^{\{1\}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathcal{M}}_{J}(\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI})({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,v_{I}\,,\ignorespaces

Definition 5.12
.

Say that a function
\,f({\boldsymbol{p}})\,
is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}

if
\,f({\boldsymbol{p}})=g(p_{2}/p_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak p_{N}/p_{N-1})\,
for an entire function

\,g(s_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak s_{N-1})\,
. Denote
\,f({\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})=g(0,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak 0)\,
.

Theorem 5.13
.

The
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
-valued function
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu
is a solution
of the joint system of dynamical differential equations (
3.13
) and
qKZ

difference equations (
3.12
). It has the form

(5.20)

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)

\displaystyle\;\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a\in J_{i}}\mskip-2.00002muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu\times{}

\displaystyle{}\,\;\times\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{N}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{a\in J_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{b\in J_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mu\frac{1}{\sin\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)}\;,

where the function
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu
is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}

and is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}\mskip 0.99998mu
. In more detail,

(5.21)

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{N}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{a\in J_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{b\in J_{j}}\mskip 2.00002mu\frac{\pi}{\Gamma\bigl(1+(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a})/\kappa\bigr)}\,\times{}

\displaystyle\mskip 2.00002mu{}\times\,\biggl(\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu+\!\!\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle\,\mb\in\Z_{\ge 0}^{N-1}\!}{\scriptstyle\mb\ne 0}}\!\mskip-2.00002mu\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\prod_{i=1}^{N\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\mskip 2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mup_{i+1}/p_{i})^{m_{i}}\mskip-0.99998mu\biggr)\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-20.00003pt

where

(5.22)

\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,v_{J}\,+\!\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle I\<\in\Il}{\scriptstyle|\si_I\<|<|\si_J\<|}}\!\!W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,v_{I}\kern-20.00003pt

is a polynomial in

are rational functions

(5.23)

\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\big|_{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}=z_{b}}=\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998mus_{a\mskip-0.99998mu,b}(J)}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\big|_{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}=z_{b}}\mskip 0.99998mu.\ignorespaces

The functions
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)
are called
the

of the dynamical equations
3.13

and
qKZ
difference equations (
3.12
).

The next theorem computes the determinant of coordinates of solutions

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
and is analogous to

[
TV6
, formula (11.23)]
.

Theorem 5.14
.

Let
n\geqslant 2
. Then

(5.24)

\displaystyle\det\mskip 0.99998mu

\displaystyle\bigl(\mskip 2.00002mu\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\kappa)\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{M}}_{J}(\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI})({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ\in\mskip 2.00002mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\mskip-0.99998mu={}

\displaystyle{}\mskip-2.00002mu=\,\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}d_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,p_{i}^{\,\smash{d^{(1)}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}},i}\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}}\;\prod_{a=1}^{n-1}\,\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998mu\biggl(\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa}{\sin\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)}\mskip 0.99998mu\biggr)^{\!d^{(2)}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}},\kern-26.00009pt

where
\;\lambda^{\{1\}}\mskip-0.99998mu=\sum_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 0.99998mu(N\mskip-2.00002mu-i)\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu
and

\;d_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,,\,d^{(1)}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mui}\,,\,d^{(2)}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
are given
by formulae  (
4.27
).

Proof.

The statement follows from Lemma
5.1
, Proposition
5.11
, and
formula (
4.26
).

Alternatively, denote by
\,F({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,
the determinant in the left-hand
side of formula (
5.24
). By Theorem
5.13
, it solves
the differential equations

\Bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial}{\partial p_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu-\mskip 2.00002mu\operatorname{tr}\mskip 0.99998muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})|_{\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\Bigr)\mskip 2.00002muF({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,=\,0\,,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where
\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})|_{\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
are the restrictions of dynamical
Hamiltonians (
3.8
) to the invariant subspace
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
. Since

\;\operatorname{tr}\mskip 0.99998muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;{\boldsymbol{q}})|_{\mskip 2.00002mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}=d^{(1)}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mui}\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\,
,
the function
\,F({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,
equals the product of powers of
\,p_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak p_{n}\mskip 0.99998mu

in the right-hand side of formula (

) multiplied by a factor that
does not depend on
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
. This factor can be found by taking the limit

p_{i+1}/p_{i}\to 0\,
for all
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
, using Theorem
5.13
.
∎

Remark 5.15
.

, the determinant

in

.

-modules.

\displaystyle\,\mskip 0.99998mu{}=\,\bigl(2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\;\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{n\mskip 0.99998mu(n-1)\mskip 2.00002mud^{(2)}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\!\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip 0.99998mu2}\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda^{\{1\}}d_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Bigl(\mskip 0.99998mue^{-\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,(n-1)\mskip 2.00002mud^{(2)}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,p_{i}^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{d^{(1)}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}},i}}\vphantom{|}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\;\prod_{j=2}^{n-1}\,j^{\mskip 0.99998mu(n-j)\mskip 2.00002mud^{(2)}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu,

.

5.5.
Levelt fundamental solution

Recall Definition
5.12
of a function
\,f({\boldsymbol{p}})\,
entire
in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}
.
The dynamical Hamiltonians
X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}),\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{n}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,
given by
(

) are entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}
and

(5.32)

X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,z_{a}\,e^{(a)}_{i,\mskip 0.99998mui}-\sum_{1\leqslant a<b\leqslant n}\mskip-2.00002mu\biggl(\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 2.00002muQ_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}^{\mskip 2.00002mua,\mskip 0.99998mub}\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\mskip-2.00002muQ_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}^{\mskip 2.00002mub,\mskip 0.99998mua}\mskip 2.00002mu\biggr)\,.\kern-1.99997pt

Notice that for
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
,

X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,v_{I}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{\mskip 0.99998mua\in I_{i}}z_{a}\mskip 0.99998muv_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu+\!\mskip-0.99998mu\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle J\in\:\Il}{\scriptstyle|\si_J\<|<|\si_I\<|}}\!\xi_{\mskip 0.99998mui,\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}\,v_{J}\,,

where the coefficients
\,\xi_{\mskip 0.99998mui,\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}\mskip 2.00002mu
take values
\;0\mskip 0.99998mu,\pm\mskip 2.00002mu1\,
.
Therefore, the eigenvalues of the restriction of the operator

\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,
on
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,
are
\,\sum_{\mskip 0.99998mua\in I_{i}}\mskip-0.99998muz_{a}\,
,

\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
. A more detailed statement is given
by Proposition
5.21
below.

Recall the function
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
,
given by (

).

Proposition 5.21
.

Given
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, we have

\;X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu=\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{\mskip 0.99998mua\in I_{i}}\mskip-2.00002muz_{a}\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, and

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\neq 0\mskip 2.00002mu
provided
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu\neq z_{b}\,
for all pairs

\,a,b\mskip 2.00002mu
such that
\,a<b\mskip 2.00002mu
and
\,\sigma_{I}^{-1}(a)>\sigma_{I}^{-1}(b)\,
.

Proof.

The first part of the statement follows from Theorem
5.13
.
The nonvanishing of
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.90417pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vartriangle$}}_{\mskip-0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
is implied by
formula (
5.22
) and Lemma
5.6
.
∎

For
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, set

\,{\boldsymbol{E}}_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})=\bigl(E^{(1)}_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}}),\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak E^{(N\mskip-0.99998mu-1)}_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr)\,
,
where
\,E^{(i)}_{I}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}})=\sum_{j=1}^{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{\mskip 0.99998mua\in I_{j}}\mskip-0.99998muz_{a}\,

is the eigenvalue of
