there is an isomorphism

(5.45)

\mu^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{\circ}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}:({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{\circ}}\mskip-2.00002mu}\mskip 2.00002mu,\qquad v\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv\,.\kern-16.00008pt

Let
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\!\mskip-0.99998mu\subset\mskip-0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
be the complement of the union of
the hyperplanes

(5.46)

z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\neq 0}\,,\qquad a,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\quad a\neq b\,.\kern-6.00006pt\ignorespaces

Denote by
\,{\mathcal{O}}\mskip 2.00002mu
the ring of functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
holomorphic in

\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
. Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
be the space of
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
-valued solutions
of dynamical differential equations (
3.13
) holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,

in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
. Both spaces
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
and
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{\circ}}\mskip-2.00002mu}\mskip 2.00002mu
are subspaces of

\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\,
,
see Proposition
5.17
and Theorem
5.22
. Let

(5.47)

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\qquad v\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv\,,\ignorespaces

be the
\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{O}}\mskip 0.99998mu
-linear extension of the map
\,\mu^{\mskip 0.99998mu\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{\circ}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
.

Define a map

(5.48)

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,,\kern-6.00006pt

(5.49)

\displaystyle[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\,\mapsto\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,W_{I}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,v_{I}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

where
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\in{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
stands for the class of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
and the functions

\,C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,
,
\,G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,
are
given by (
5.41
), (
5.42
), respectively.
By Proposition
5.23
, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
sends the class

\,Y\!\in{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
to the principal term of the solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{Y}\mskip 0.99998mu

of the joint system of dynamical differential equations (
3.13
) and

qKZ
difference equations (
3.12
).

Proposition 5.24
.

The map
\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,

is well-defined and the following diagram is commutative,

(5.50)

\textstyle{{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.43054pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle K}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\;\;\,
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}

\;\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\textstyle{\!\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\,}

Proof.

Poles of the sum in the right-hand side of (
5.49
) are at most those
of the function
\,G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;h;\kappa)\,
and, therefore, in addition to
hyperplanes (
5.46
) can occur only at the hyperplanes
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu=z_{b}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

\,a\neq b
. However, the sums

\sum_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};\kappa)\,W_{I}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\kern-8.00003pt\ignorespaces

are regular at the hyperplanes
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu=z_{b}\mskip 2.00002mu
for all
\,a\neq b\,

by the standard reasoning. Hence, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is well-defined.

The commutativity of diagram (
5.50
) follows from
Proposition
5.23
.
∎

Remark 5.25
.

Since
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
,
and
\,\bigl(\det\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is entire
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
, see (
5.34
), the inverse matrix

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
.
Therefore, for every
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi\in\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, its principal term

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}\!=\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}{}^{\raise 1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu-1\mskip-2.00002mu$}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi\,
,
defined by (
5.40
), belongs to
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,
,
and we have an isomorphism
\,\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\!\to({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,
,

\;\Psi\mapsto\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}
. The inverse map, equals
\,\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
, see (
5.47
),
so that
\,\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip-2.00002mu=\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\smash{\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{\circ}}\mskip-2.00002mu}\mskip-0.99998mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,
.

5.7.
Example
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,

Throughout this section, let
\,N=2\,
,
\,n\geqslant 2\,
,
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,
.
Like in Section
4.10
, denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]
the element

\bigl(\{a\},\{\mskip 0.99998mu1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1,a+1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\mskip 0.99998mu\}\bigr)\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}
,
The space
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
has a basis
\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mu1\mskip 0.99998mu]},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak v_{[\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu
,
where
\,v_{[a]}\mskip-0.99998mu=v_{2}^{\otimes(a-1)}\mskip-0.99998mu\otimes v_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu\otimes v_{2}^{\otimes(n-a)}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
Clearly
\,e_{1,1}^{(a)}v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}=\mskip 0.99998mu\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,
and

\,e_{2,2}^{(a)}v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}=(1-\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub})\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,
.

The
qKZ
operators
\,K^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak K^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{n}\mskip 2.00002mu
, see (
3.4
), are

(5.51)

\displaystyle\kern 20.00003ptK^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu={}

\displaystyle\,\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a-1}+\kappa)\bigr)^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mua-1)}\dots\,\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{1}+\kappa)\bigr)^{(a,1)}\times{}

\displaystyle{}\mskip-2.00002mu\times\,p_{1}^{e_{1,1}^{(a)}}\mskip 0.99998mup_{2}^{e_{2,2}^{(a)}}\,\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{n})\bigr)^{(a,n)}\dots\,\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a+1})\bigr)^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mua+1)}\,.\kern-20.00003pt

The
\mskip 0.99998muR\mskip 0.99998mu
-matrices in the right-hand side
preserve the subspace
({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\subset\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}
, acting there as follows,

\displaystyle\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z)\bigr)^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}v_{[a]}\,=\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}-z\mskip 0.99998muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\kern 16.00008pt\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z)\bigr)^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,=\,v_{[a]}\,,\kern-1.99997pt

\displaystyle\bigl(R^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(z)\bigr)^{(a,\mskip 0.99998mub)}v_{[c]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002muv_{[c]}\,,\qquad c\neq a,b\,.\kern-10.00002pt

The
qKZ
difference equations (
3.12
) are

(5.52)

f(z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a}+\kappa,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n};{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,K^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\mskip 2.00002mu.\kern-10.00002pt

The dynamical Hamiltonians
\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}\mskip 2.00002mu
, see (
3.8
),
act on
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
as follows

(5.53)

\displaystyle X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mu1\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,z_{1}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mu1\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu+\mskip 2.00002mu\frac{p_{2}}{p_{1}}\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,

\displaystyle X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,z_{a}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu+\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua-1\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,

\displaystyle a=2,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\kern-20.00003pt

\displaystyle X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 2.00002muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002mu+\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,z_{c}\Bigr)\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\qquad

\displaystyle b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\kern-20.00003pt

and the dynamical differential equations (
2.7
) are

(5.54)

\displaystyle\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial}{\partial\mskip 0.99998mup_{1}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial}{\partial\mskip 0.99998mup_{2}}\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002mu\Psi({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-10.00002pt

In this section, we use the variable
\,t=t^{(1)}_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu
.
The substitution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{t}}=\Sigma_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}\mskip 2.00002mu
reads as
\,t\mskip 0.99998mu=z_{a}\,
.
The weight functions are

(5.55)

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,(t-z_{b})\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-20.00003pt

The permutations
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{[1]},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\sigma_{[n]}\,
are

\sigma_{[a]}(1)=a\,,\qquad\sigma_{[a]}(b)=b-1\,,\quad b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1\,,\qquad\sigma_{[a]}(b)=b\,,\quad b=a+1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\ignorespaces

and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu|\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{[a]}\mskip 0.99998mu|=a-1\,
. We have

(5.56)

\displaystyle W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{b=1}^{a-1}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})\,,

\displaystyle W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu0\,,\qquad b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1\,,

cf. Lemma
5.6
.

The functions
\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, see (
5.5
), are

(5.57)

{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{c=a+1}^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu(t-z_{b})\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Set

\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle b=1}{\scriptstyle b\ne a}}^{n}\,(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-20.00003pt

Then
\,\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})=R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{[a]}})\,,
, where the function

\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
is given by (
4.8
).
Biorthogonality relations (
5.7
) become

\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,\frac{W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[b]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}})}{\bar{R}_{c}({\boldsymbol{z}})}\,=\,\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,,\qquad\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[c]}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[c]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,\bar{R}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,.\kern-16.00008pt

The master function, see (
5.8
), is

(5.58)

\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,(p_{2}/\kappa)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,(\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mun}p_{1}/p_{2})^{\mskip 0.99998mut/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(t-z_{a})/\kappa\bigr)\,.\kern-13.99995pt

The
hypergeometric solutions (
4.20
) of the joint system of
differential equations (
4.68
) and
difference equations (
4.66
) have the form

\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\frac{1}{\kappa}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\,\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\;W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\kern-16.00008pt\ignorespaces

where the residues of the master function are

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,={}

\displaystyle{}\,=\,\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu(\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}p_{1})^{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu(p_{2}/\kappa)^{\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{c=1,\mskip 2.00002muc\neq a}^{n}\mskip-0.99998muz_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\frac{(-\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{-n}p_{2}/p_{1})^{\mskip 0.99998mul}}{l\mskip 0.99998mu!}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne a}}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(-\mskip 0.99998mul+(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c})/\kappa\bigr)\,.

Determinant formula for coordinates of the
hypergeometric solutions, see (
5.24
), is

(5.59)

\displaystyle\det\mskip 0.99998mu\biggl(\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{a}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\kappa)\mskip-2.00002mu\biggr)_{\!\mskip-2.00002mua,\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu=1}^{\!\mskip-0.99998mun}\mskip-0.99998mu={}

\displaystyle\mskip 0.99998mu{}=\,\pi^{\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n-1)/2}\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n+1)/2}\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mup_{1}\mskip 2.00002mup_{2}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\prod_{a=1}^{n-1}\,\prod_{b=a+1}^{n}\,\frac{1}{\sin\bigl(\pi\mskip 2.00002mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)}\;.\kern-10.00002pt

By formula (
5.55
) and the Vandermonde determinant formula,
equality (
4.72
) transforms to

(5.60)

\displaystyle\det\mskip 0.99998mu\biggl(\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,

\displaystyle\operatorname{Res}_{\mskip 2.00002mut\mskip 0.99998mu=\;z_{c}-\mskip 0.99998mul\kappa}\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mut^{\mskip 0.99998mua-1}\mskip 0.99998mue^{-\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\mskip 0.99998mu(b-1)\mskip 2.00002mut/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\,\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)\mskip-2.00002mu\biggr)_{\!\mskip-2.00002mua,\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu=1}^{\!\mskip-0.99998mun}\mskip-0.99998mu={}\kern-20.00003pt

\displaystyle{}=\,{}

\displaystyle\bigl(2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip-0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n-1)/2}\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu(n+1)/2}\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mue^{-\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\mskip 0.99998mu(n-1)}p_{1}\mskip 2.00002mup_{2}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu.\kern-20.00003pt

Let
\,\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}=\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{1,1}\,
. For a Laurent polynomial
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
,
we have

\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,P(e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\gamma\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 0.99998mue^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{n}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu)\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

The solution
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}\mskip 2.00002mu
of differential equations (
5.54
) and
difference equations (
5.52
) corresponding to
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
is

(5.61)

\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{a};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,.\kern-11.99998pt\ignorespaces

By Proposition
5.17
, it is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
and is holomorphic in
\,p_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002mup_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu
provided
branches of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998mup_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu
and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\log\mskip 0.99998mup_{2}\mskip 0.99998mu
are fixed.

The solution
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
can be written as an integral
over a suitable contour
\,C\mskip 2.00002mu
encircling the poles of the product

\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\mskip-0.99998mu\Gamma\bigl({(t-z_{a})/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\bigr)\,
counterclockwise,

(5.62)

\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\frac{1}{2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\kappa}\;\int\limits_{\!\!C\,}\mskip-0.99998mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\Phi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,v_{[a]}\,d\mskip 0.99998mut\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

For instance, the integral can be taken over the parabola

C\,=\,\{\,\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu\bigl(\mskip 0.99998muA-s^{2}\mskip-2.00002mu+s\mskip 2.00002mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\bigr)\ \,|\ \,s\in{\mathbb{R}}\,\}\,,\ignorespaces

where
\,A\,
is a sufficiently large positive real number.
Formula (
4.75
) can be used to give an alternative proof of
analytic properties of the function
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
.

Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
be the space of solutions of the joint system of dynamical
differential equations (
5.54
) and
qKZ
difference
equations (
5.52
)
spanned over
\,{\mathbb{C}}\,
by the functions
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

corresponding to Laurent polynomials
\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
.
The space
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
is a
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 0.99998mu
-module
with
\,f(\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
acting as multiplication by

\,f(e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{n}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu)\,
.

For
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,
, the algebra
\,{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
, see (
5.28
),
can be presented as follows

(5.63)

{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}^{\pm 1}\mskip-2.00002mu,\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 2.00002mu\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}-\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{a})\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu0\,\Bigr\rangle\,.\kern-16.00008pt\ignorespaces

The function
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
depends only on the class
of the Laurent polynomial
\,P\mskip 0.99998mu
in
\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
.
The assignment
\,P\mapsto\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{P}\mskip 2.00002mu
defines the homomorphism

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\circ}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu:{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu\mathscr{S}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,,\qquad Y\mskip-0.99998mu\mapsto\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{Y}\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

of
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 0.99998mu
-modules. Formula (
5.60
)
implies that the homomorphism
\,\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\circ}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
is an isomorphism.

The algebra
\,{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
is the equivariant
\mskip 2.00002muK\mskip-2.00002mu
-theory algebra

\,K_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})\mskip 2.00002mu
of the projective space
\,{\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}
,
see the notation in Section
6
.

Recall Definition
5.12
of a function
\,f({\boldsymbol{p}})\,
entire
in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}
.
For example, the dynamical Hamiltonians
\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
see (
5.53
), are entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}_{\!{\scriptscriptstyle\div}}
and
\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,
,

\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\,
act on
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,
as follows

X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,z_{a}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu+\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua-1\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\qquad X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\mskip 2.00002muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu\Bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu-\mskip 2.00002muX^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\mathchoice{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.68889pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.55199pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38748pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})\mskip 2.00002mu+\sum_{b=1}^{n}\,z_{b}\Bigr)\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

where
\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mu0\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip-0.99998mu=0\,
.

The Levelt fundamental solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
,
see (
5.33
), of differential equations (
