) is

\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}};{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\;p_{1}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\smash{X^{\raise 0.43054pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})}/\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mup_{2}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\smash{X^{\raise 0.43054pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{2}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.48222pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.38638pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}{\raise 0.27122pt\hbox to0.0pt{$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.00002mu\wr$\hss}0}})}/\kappa}\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-13.99995pt\ignorespaces

where the
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\operatorname{End}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigl(({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\bigr)
-valued function

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is as follows,

(5.64)

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998muv_{[\mskip 0.99998mub\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,v_{[a]}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\,\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mua-b}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{J}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub,\mskip 2.00002mul}({\boldsymbol{z}}/\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(\mskip-0.99998mu-\kappa)^{-\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu}p_{2}/p_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu\bigr)^{l}\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub,\mskip 2.00002mul}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\sum_{c=1}^{n}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu-l\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[b]}(z_{c};{\boldsymbol{z}})\;\frac{(-1)^{n-1}}{l\mskip 0.99998mu!}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle d=1}{\scriptstyle d\ne c}}^{n}\,\prod_{m=0}^{l}\,\frac{1}{z_{d}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu+m}\;.\kern-10.00002pt

Furthermore, one has
\;\det\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)=1\,\mskip 0.99998mu
and

\;\det\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mup_{1}\mskip 2.00002mup_{2}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\bigr)^{\sum_{a=1}^{n}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-2.00002mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu
.

For a solution
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
of dynamical differential equations
(
5.54
), its principal term is

\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\ignorespaces

see (
5.40
). The principal term of the solution

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
, corresponding to a Laurent polynomial

\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
, see (
5.43
), equals

(5.65)

\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[a]}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\;\kappa^{\left.\left(\mskip-0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998muz_{b}\mskip 0.99998mu-\sum_{c=1}^{n}z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)\mskip-0.99998mu\right/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne b}}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c})/\kappa\bigr)\,.\kern-8.99994pt

Let
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
be the complement of the union of the hyperplanes

\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\neq 0}\,
,
\,a,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
,
\,a\neq b\,
,
cf. (
5.46
). Denote by
\,{\mathcal{O}}\,
the ring of functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,

holomorphic in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
. The map

(5.66)

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\,,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle{[P\mskip 0.99998mu]}\,\mapsto\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{a=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muv_{[a]}\,\sum_{b=1}^{n}\mskip 2.00002muW^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(z_{b};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\;\kappa^{\left.\left(\mskip-0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998muz_{b}\mskip 0.99998mu-\sum_{c=1}^{n}z_{c}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)\mskip-0.99998mu\right/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\,\prod_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle c=1}{\scriptstyle c\ne b}}^{n}\,\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{c})/\kappa\bigr)\kern-10.00002pt

sends the class
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\in{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
to the principal term of the solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}\mskip 0.99998mu

of the joint system of differential equations (
5.54
) and difference
equations (
5.52
).

Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}
be the space of
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
-valued solutions of dynamical
differential equations (
5.54
) holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
.
The space
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is a subspace of
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}
.

Since the matrix
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in

\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
, and

\,\bigl(\det\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is entire in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
,
the inverse matrix
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)^{-1}\,
is also holomorphic
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
in
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\,
. Thus the map

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\mskip 2.00002mu\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}},\qquad v\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muv\,,\ignorespaces

gives an isomorphism of
\,{\mathcal{O}}\,
-modules.
Furthermore, the following diagram is commutative,

\textstyle{{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu{\raise 0.43054pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{K}}^{\circ}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\;\;\,
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}

\;\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
\textstyle{\!\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu}

see Proposition
5.24
.

6.
Equations for partial flag varieties

6.1.
Equivariant cohomology of partial flag varieties

Consider the partial flag variety
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
parametrizing chains of subspaces

0\,=\,F_{0}\subset F_{1}\subset\allowbreak\dots\subset\allowbreak F_{N}\mskip 2.00002mu=\,{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\kern-13.99995pt

with
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\dim F_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu/F_{i-1}=\lambda_{i}\,
,
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Given a basis of
\,{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
, the group
\,GL_{n}({\mathbb{C}})\,
acts on
\,{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
.
Let
T\subset GL_{n}({\mathbb{C}})
be the torus of diagonal matrices.
Let
\,z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n}\,
the Chern roots corresponding to the factors of
\,T\,
,
and for
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
, let
\,\gamma_{i,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}\,
be the Chern
roots of the bundle over
{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
with fiber
\,F_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu/F_{i-1}\,
. Denote

\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip-0.99998mu=(\gamma_{1,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{1,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{1}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{N,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{N\mskip-2.00002mu,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{N}})\,

and
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,=\,(z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n})
, cf. (
4.40
). Let
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
be the
space of polynomials in
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 2.00002mu
symmetric in
\,\gamma_{i,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}

for each
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Consider the equivariant cohomology algebra
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. Then

(6.1)

H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,(u-\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj})\,=\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(u-z_{a})\Bigr\rangle\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where
\,u\,
is a formal variable.
For a polynomial
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
,
denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]\,
its class in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.
Notice that for each
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
,

(6.2)

c_{1}(E_{i})\,=\,[\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma_{i,1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{i}}]\kern-3.99994pt\ignorespaces

is the equivariant first Chern class of the vector bundle
\,E_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu

over
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
with fiber
\,F_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu/F_{i-1}\,
.

Given a point
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
, consider the algebra

(6.3)

\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,(u-\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj})\,=\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(u-z^{0}_{a})\Bigr\rangle\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

The evaluation map

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\to{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mapsto f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\,
, identifies

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
with the quotient

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})/\langle{\boldsymbol{z}}={\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip 0.99998mu\rangle\,
. Denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}

the class of
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
in

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
.

Recall the polynomials
\,V_{\mskip-0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
,
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, given by
formula (
A.6
).

Lemma 6.1
.

The algebra
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})
is a free module over

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({pt};{\mathbb{C}})={\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]\mskip 0.99998mu
generated by the classes

\,[\mskip 0.99998muV_{\mskip-0.99998muI}\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
,
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
. For every
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
,
the classes
\,[\mskip 0.99998muV_{\mskip-0.99998muI}\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
give a basis of
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
. In particular,

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\dim\mskip 0.99998mu\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}=\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu!/(\lambda_{1}!\ldots\lambda_{N}!\mskip 0.99998mu)\mskip 2.00002mu

for every
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}
.

Proof.

The statement follows from Propositions
A.2
and
A.3
.
∎

Recall the polynomial
\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, see (
4.8
). Let

(6.4)

V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{a=\lambda^{(i)}+1}^{n}\!(\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{a})\,,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

cf. (
A.1
). Then
\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})=R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
and

\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};{\boldsymbol{z}})=0\,\mskip 0.99998mu
for
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\neq I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
.

For a polynomial
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, consider the restrictions

f({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
.

Lemma 6.2
.

The map
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\to\bigoplus_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998muz\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
,

\,[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]\,\mapsto\,\bigl(f({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,,\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\bigr)\mskip 0.99998mu

is well-defined and injective. A collection

\,\bigl(F_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,,\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\bigr)\,
belongs to the image of this map
if and only if for any
I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}
and any transposition
\,s_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\,
,

(6.5)

F_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\big|_{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}=z_{b}}=\,F_{s_{a\mskip-0.99998mu,b}(I)}({\boldsymbol{z}})\big|_{\mskip 0.99998muz_{a}=z_{b}}\mskip 0.99998mu.

For a collection
\,\bigl(F_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,,\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\bigr)\,

obeying (
6.5
), the function

(6.6)

f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{F_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

is a polynomial and
\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};{\boldsymbol{z}})=F_{I}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,

for every
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Proof.

Straightforward.
∎

Lemma 6.3
.

For any
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
, the function

(6.7)

{\mathcal{E}}\langle f\rangle({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\,\frac{f({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}},{\boldsymbol{z}})}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

is a polynomial depending only on the class of
\,f\,

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

Proof.

Straightforward.
∎

The induced map

(6.8)

{\mathcal{E}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,\to\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]\,,\qquad[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{E}}\langle f\rangle\,,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

is the
equivariant integration map on

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

Identify
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
with the subspace of

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
of polynomials not depending
on
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. For
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, denote

\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\langle f\rangle={\mathcal{E}}\langle f\rangle({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\,
.

Lemma 6.4
.

For any
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\mskip 2.00002mu
and

\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
, we have

{\mathcal{E}}\langle f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\rangle({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\,=\,{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\langle f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\rangle\,.\kern-10.00002pt

Proof.

Straightforward.
∎

By Lemmas
6.3
,
6.4
, for every
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
,
there is a well-defined map

(6.9)

{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}:\mskip 0.99998mu\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu\to\,{\mathbb{C}}\,,\qquad{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}:[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\,{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\langle f\rangle\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

called the
integration map on

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
.

6.2.
Stable envelope map

Recall the functions
\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,

\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, see (
5.1
), (
5.5
).
Let
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
be
the polynomials respectively obtained from
\,{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,

W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
by the substitution

(6.10)

(\mskip 0.99998mut^{(i)}_{1}\mskip-2.00002mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak t^{(i)}_{\lambda^{(i)}}\mskip 0.99998mu)\,=\,(\gamma_{1,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{1,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{1}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{i\mskip 0.99998mu,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\gamma_{i\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}})\,,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Notice that

(6.11)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,,\qquad\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002muW^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,.\kern-11.99998pt\ignorespaces

By Lemma
5.2
and formula (
6.4
), we have

(6.12)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 0.99998muV_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,,\qquad

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{0}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002mu1\,,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 2.00002mu1\,,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{0}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 2.00002mu

\displaystyle{}=\mskip 0.99998muV_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{0}}\mskip-0.99998mu)\,,\kern-10.00002pt

where
\,\sigma_{0}\mskip 2.00002mu
is the longest permutation,
\,\sigma_{0}(a)=n+1-a\,
,

\;a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
. By Proposition
B.5
, the polynomials

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
coincide with
the
A\mskip 0.99998mu
-type double Schubert polynomials
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,

(6.13)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma_{\mskip-2.00002mu\sigma_{\mskip-0.99998mu0}\mskip-0.99998mu(I)}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{0}})\,,\kern 16.00008pt\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma_{I}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Lemma 6.5
.

For any
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

and

(6.14)

\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

Proof.

Fix a generic
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
and consider both sides of each formula as functions
of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu
. Formulae (
6.12
) and Lemma
5.4
imply that

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,

are in the span of the polynomials
\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\mskip-0.99998mu\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)\,
,

\,J\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
. Then the statement follows from formulae (
6.6
),
(
6.13
).
∎

Lemma 6.6
.

The polynomials
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu
and
\;\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu

are biorthogonal,

(6.15)

{\mathcal{E}}\langle\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\rangle\,=\,\delta_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}\,.\ignorespaces

Proof.

The statement follows from formulae (
6.7
), (
6.13
),
and Lemma
5.7
.
∎

Lemma
6.15
is equivalent to the orthogonality relation for the Schubert
polynomials.

Let
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}\mskip 2.00002mu
be the classes of

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. Define the
stable envelope map
by the rule

(6.16)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,\to\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,,\qquad v_{I}\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}\mskip 2.00002mu,\quad I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Lemma 6.7
.

The map
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
is an isomorphism of free
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]
-modules.

Proof.

The statement follows from Lemma
6.6
.
For any
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
, one has

\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip-0.99998mu:\mskip 2.00002mu[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]\,\mapsto\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{J\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathcal{E}}\langle f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\rangle\,v_{J}\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

∎

Let
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}
,
\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}
be the classes of

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\,

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
. Consider the map

(6.17)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998mu({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\to\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu,\qquad v_{I}\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu,\quad I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Lemma 6.8
.

For every
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
, the classes
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, give a basis of
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
That is, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}
is an isomorphism.

Proof.

The statement follows from Lemmas
6.6
,
6.4
.
For any
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, one has

\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip-2.00002mu\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip-0.99998mu:\mskip 2.00002mu[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{J\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\langle\mskip 0.99998muf(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0})\rangle\,v_{J}\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

∎

Consider a polynomial

(6.18)

{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\prod_{k=1}^{\lambda_{i+1}}\,(t^{(i)}_{j}\mskip-2.00002mu-\gamma_{i+1,\mskip 0.99998muk})\,.\ignorespaces

and its image
\,\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,
in

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu]\otimes\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. Clearly, for any
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip-0.99998muJ\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
,

(6.19)

{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(\Sigma_{I}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{J})\,=\,\delta_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

Theorem 6.9
.

We have

\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,=\,\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}\mskip 2.00002mu.

Proof.

The statement follows from
[
TV6
, Proposition 9.2]
and Lemmas

5.1
,
5.2
.
∎

Remark 6.10
.

According to formulae (
6.13
), the classes
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}\mskip 0.99998mu
,

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}\mskip 0.99998mu
,
\,\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
, are the equivariant fundamental classes
of the Schubert subvarieties in
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
defined for the opposite orderings
of the chosen basis of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
in the standard way,
see
[
O
, Section 4.1]
, cf.
[
RV
, Section 6]
. On the equivariant
fundamental classes of Schubert varieties see, in particular,
[
R
,
FRW
]
.

Remark 6.11
.

Stable envelope maps for Nakajima quiver varieties were introduced in

[
MO
]
. They were defined in
[
MO
]
geometrically in terms of the
associated torus action. The map
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
given by formula
(
6.16
) is the limit as
\,h\to\infty\,
of the stable envelope map of

[
MO
]
associated with the cotangent bundle
\,{T^{*}\mskip-2.00002mu{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
of the partial flag
variety. In
[
RTV1
]
, the stable envelope map for
\,{T^{*}\mskip-2.00002mu{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is described
in terms of the Chern roots of the bundles
\,{\boldsymbol{F}}_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak{\boldsymbol{F}}_{N-1}\,
over

\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
with fibers
\,F_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak F_{N-1}
, respectively.

Remark 6.12
.

Unlike the stable envelope map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
defined by (
6.16
),
the stable envelope map for the cotangent bundle
\,{T^{*}\mskip-2.00002mu{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is not
an isomorphism of free
{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]
-modules
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
and

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{\smash{T\times\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\times}}}\mskip-0.99998mu({T^{*}\mskip-2.00002mu{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, but only an embedding.

6.3.
Quantum multiplication

The quantum multiplication in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, see for example

[
M
,
BM
]
, is a deformation of the multiplication in

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
depending on quantum parameters. In the notation of

[
M
]
, the quantum parameters are
\,q^{\mskip 2.00002mu\alpha_{i}}\mskip-2.00002mu
,
\,i=,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
.
In the notation of this paper, we have
\,q^{\mskip 2.00002mu\alpha_{i}}\!=p_{i+1}/p_{i}\,
.

For
\,Y\!\in\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, denote by
\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip-0.99998mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 2.00002mu
the operator
of quantum multiplication by
\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip 2.00002mu
. Recall that the operators
\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip-0.99998mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 2.00002mu

are
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]\mskip 0.99998mu
-module endomorphisms of
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

The dynamical operators
\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}),\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muN}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,
,
see (
3.8
), are linear functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. Thus their action
on
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
extends to the
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu]\mskip 0.99998mu
-linear action
on
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,
.

For
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
, denote

\,D_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu=\gamma_{i,1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, cf. (
6.2
).

Theorem 6.13
.

The isomorphism
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
intertwines the dynamical operators

\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}),\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muN}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 0.99998mu
acting on
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\mskip 2.00002mu
and
the operators of quantum multiplication
\,[D_{1}]\mskip 2.00002mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak[D_{N}]\mskip 2.00002mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu

acting on
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
,

(6.20)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu\circ X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,=\,[D_{i}]\mskip 2.00002mu{*_{\boldsymbol{p}}}\mskip 0.99998mu\circ\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

Proof.

Theorem 6.4 in
[
M
]
describes the quantum multiplication
in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
by the equivariant divisor classes.
In the notation of this paper those classes equal

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 2.00002mu\sigma_{0}\mskip 2.00002mus_{\lambda^{(i)}\!,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}\,
,

\,\mskip 0.99998mui=1,\dots,N-1\,
, where
\,\sigma_{0}\,
is the longest permutation.
By formulae (
6.16
), (
5.5
), (
5.3
),

(6.21)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 2.00002mu\sigma_{0}\mskip 2.00002mus_{\lambda^{(i)}\!,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}})}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,D_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak D_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{n-\lambda^{(i)}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}\mskip-0.99998mu-\allowbreak\dots-\allowbreak z_{n}\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Comparing formula (
3.8
) term by term with formula (6.1) in
[
M
]

yields formula (
6.20
) for
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
. Formula (
6.20
) for

\,i=N\mskip 2.00002mu
holds since
\,[\mskip 0.99998muD_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak D_{N}\mskip 0.99998mu]=z_{1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak z_{n}\,
in

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
and

\,X_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak X_{N}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})=z_{1}\mskip-2.00002mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak z_{n}\,
.
Recall that for each
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
, the operator
\,z_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 2.00002mu

is the ordinary multiplication by
\,z_{i}\,
,
∎

Since the operators of quantum multiplication act
\,{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\mskip 0.99998mu
-linearly on

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, the quantum multiplication in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,

can be restricted to
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}
. Denote by

\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip-0.99998mu{*}_{{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu
the operator
of quantum multiplication by
\mskip 2.00002muY\!\in\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Corollary 6.14
.

For every
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
, the isomorphism
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu

intertwines the dynamical operators

\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0};{\boldsymbol{p}}),\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muN}({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0};{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 0.99998mu
acting on
({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
and
the operators of quantum multiplication

\,[D_{1}]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu{*}_{{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak[D_{N}]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu{*}_{{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}

acting on
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
,

(6.22)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu\circ X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0};{\boldsymbol{p}})\,=\,{[D_{i}]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu{*}_{{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}}\mskip 0.99998mu\circ\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

6.4.
Differential and difference equations

Consider
the space
\,{{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\!\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}}
with coordinates
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
.
By Lemma
6.1
, we have a trivial vector bundle

\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-2.00002mu\to\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\!\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}
with fiber over a point

\,({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu,{\boldsymbol{p}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu0})\,
given by
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
.
Let
\,U_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-2.00002mu\to\,{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\!\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}
be the trivial vector bundle with fiber

\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
.

Lemma 6.15
.

The map

\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\scriptscriptstyle\lozenge}}:\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip-0.99998muU_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998muH_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
,

\,({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu,{\boldsymbol{p}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip-2.00002mu,v)\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu({\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu,{\boldsymbol{p}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip-2.00002mu,\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002muv)\mskip 2.00002mu
,
is an isomorphism of vector bundles.

Proof.

The statement follows from Lemma
6.8
.
∎

The
equivariant quantum differential equations
for sections
of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
is a system of differential equations

(6.23)

\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial f}{\partial\mskip 0.99998mup_{i}}\,=\,{[D_{i}]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 2.00002mu{*}_{{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}}\mskip 2.00002muf\,,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where
\,\kappa\,
is the parameter of the equations. By Corollary
6.14
,
the isomorphism
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\scriptscriptstyle\lozenge}}\,
identifies equations (
6.23
)
with the limiting dynamical differential equation (
3.13
) for sections
of
\,U_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
,

(6.24)

\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial f}{\partial\mskip 0.99998mup_{i}}\,=\,X^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{i}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\mskip 2.00002muf\,,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu.\kern-18.99995pt\ignorespaces

Furthermore, the isomorphism
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\scriptscriptstyle\lozenge}}\,
and the limiting
qKZ

equations (
3.12
) for sections of
\,U_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}
define the
qKZ

difference equations in cohomology

(6.25)

f(z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu+\kappa,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n};{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,K^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,,\qquad a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where for each
\,a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
, and fixed
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
,
the operator
\,K^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is a map of fibers,

\displaystyle K^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu:\mskip 2.00002mu\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 2.00002mu\to\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu(z_{1},\mskip 0.99998mu\ldots\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002muz_{a}+\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu\ldots\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002muz_{n})}\,,\kern-11.00008pt

\displaystyle K^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}},\mskip 0.99998mu(z_{1}\mskip-2.00002mu,\mskip 0.99998mu\ldots\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002muz_{a}+\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu\ldots\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002muz_{n})}\mskip 0.99998mu\circ\mskip 0.99998muK^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu\circ\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}},\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip-0.99998mu)^{-1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-10.00002pt

and the operator
\,K^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{a}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is given by (
3.4
).

Theorem 6.16
.

Quantum differential equations (
6.23
) and
qKZ
difference equations
(
6.25
) define a compatible system of differential and difference equations
for sections of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

The statement follows from Theorem

.

6.5.
Solutions with values in cohomology

Recall the isomorphism

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\scriptscriptstyle\lozenge}}:U_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\to\mskip 0.99998muH_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
of vector bundles,
see Lemma
6.15
. For any function
\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

with values in
\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
, that is, a section of
\,U_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,
, denote by

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002muf\,
the corresponding section of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
with values

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002muf({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\bigl(f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)\,.\ignorespaces

Recall the solutions
\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

of the joint system of limiting dynamical differential equations (
3.13
)
and
qKZ
difference equations (
3.12
) labeled by Laurent polynomials

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}};\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
, see formula (
5.26
) and Proposition
5.17
.

Theorem 6.17
.

The isomorphism
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\scriptscriptstyle\lozenge}}\mskip 2.00002mu
transforms the solutions

\,\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu
of equations (

), (
3.12
)
to solutions of the joint system of quantum differential equations and
qKZ

difference equations (
6.23
), (

). Namely, for each

\mskip 2.00002muP\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\,
, the function

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

(

), (

).

and the definition of the

).
∎

In this section we work out the geometric interpretation of the obtained
solutions. Notice that it would be interesting to relate our solutions with
constructions in
[
C
]
, where Mellin-Barnes integral representations
of solutions of the quantum differential equations were constructed for
a class of smooth projective varieties.

For
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
, let
\,\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{1,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{1\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{i}}
be virtual line
bundles such that
\,\bigoplus_{j=1}^{\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}=\mskip 0.99998muF_{i}/F_{i-1}\,
,
and
\,\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
correspond to the factors of the torus
\,T\,
.
Denote

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}\mskip-2.00002mu=(\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{1,1}^{\pm 1}\mskip 0.99998mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{N\mskip-2.00002mu,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{N}}^{\pm 1})\,

and
\,\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}\mskip-2.00002mu=(\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{1}^{\pm 1}\mskip-2.00002mu,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{n}^{\pm 1})\,
, cf. (
4.40
).
Let
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
be the space of Laurent polynomials in

\,\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}
symmetric in
\,\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}
for each
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Consider the equivariant
\mskip 2.00002muK\mskip-2.00002mu
-theory algebra
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. Then

(6.26)

K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,(u-\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj})\,=\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(u-\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{a})\Bigr\rangle\,,\kern-16.00008pt\ignorespaces

where
\,u\,
is a formal variable. That is,
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
coincides
with the algebra
\,{\mathcal{K}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, see (
5.28
).
For a Laurent polynomial
\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1};\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}^{\pm 1}]\,
,
denote by
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
its class in
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

Lemma 6.18
.

The algebra
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})
is a free module over
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\,
.

Proof.

The statement follows from Propositions
A.2
and
A.3
.
∎

Denote by
\,\mathscr{S}^{\scriptscriptstyle K}_{\mskip-2.00002muH_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
the space spanned over
\,{\mathbb{C}}\,
by the solutions

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

.

is well-defined, cf. (

-modules.

In Section

we introduced the Levelt fundamental solution

Recall the function
\,A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{t}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

,
\;a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,
.
For
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\,
and
\,{\boldsymbol{m}}=(m_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak m_{N-1})\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{N\mskip-0.99998mu-1}
, set

(6.30)

\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{i=1}^{N-1}\!\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle\lb\in\:\smash{\Z^{\:\la\+1}}\!\vp{|^{1^1}}}{\scriptstyle\!|\:\lb^{(i)}\<|=\:m_i}}\mskip-0.99998mu\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-2.00002mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-2.00002mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{\hbox{$A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}$}\mskip-0.99998mu(\Sigma_{J};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;,\kern-10.00002pt

where
\;|\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{l}}^{(i)}|=\mskip 0.99998mul^{\mskip 0.99998mu(i)}_{j}\!+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\mskip 0.99998mul^{\mskip 0.99998mu(i)}_{\lambda^{(i)}}\,
,

\;i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N-1\,
. cf. (
5.36
), (

).
The functions
\,{\mathcal{J}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{l}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002mu

are polynomials in
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu
and rational functions in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}},\kappa\,
.

Proposition 6.19
.

The functions
\,\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu
are regular
if
\,{z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\not\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\neq\mskip 0.99998mu0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
for all
\,a\neq b\,
,
and have at most simple poles at the hyperplanes

\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\neq\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Proof.

Recall the functions
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
and

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
, see (
5.37
), (
5.18
),
respectively. By formulae (
5.36
), (
5.37
), (
6.14
),
(
5.7
), (

), we have

(6.31)

\displaystyle\kern-3.00003pt\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{}\mskip 0.99998mu

\displaystyle\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 2.00002muJ}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,={}

\displaystyle\!\mskip-0.99998mu{}=\,\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\!\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{{\boldsymbol{m}}\mskip 0.99998mu\in\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{N-1}\!}\!\mskip-0.99998mu\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(-\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa)^{-\lambda_{i}-\lambda_{i+1}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i+1}/p_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 0.99998mum_{i}}.\kern-20.00003pt

Formula (
5.38
) and Theorem
5.22
imply that the functions

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\bullet$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
are regular if

\,{z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\not\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\neq\mskip 0.99998mu0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
for all
\,a\neq b\,
, and have
at most simple poles at the hyperplanes
\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\neq\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
Thus the statement of Proposition
6.19
follows from formula
(
6.31
).
∎

of the bundle

6.6.
The map
\,{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\,

Let
\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\!\mskip-0.99998mu\subset\mskip-0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
be the complement of the union of
the hyperplanes

(6.36)

z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b}\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\neq 0}\,,\qquad a,b=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,,\quad a\neq b\,.\kern-6.00006pt\ignorespaces

Denote by
\,{\mathcal{O}}\mskip 2.00002mu
the ring of functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
holomorphic in

\,L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
, and by
\,{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu
the space of sections of the bundle

\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
and not depending on

\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
. The stable envelope map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
induces an isomorphism

\,({\mathbb{C}}^{N})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}}{\mathcal{O}}\to{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

\;f\mapsto\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002muf\,
.

Let
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
be the space of sections of
H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
that are solutions
of quantum differential equations (
6.24
) holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,

for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
. The space
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
is a counterpart
of the space
\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
of solutions of limiting dynamical differential
equations (
3.13
) introduced in Section
5.6
.
The map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
induces an isomorphism
\,\mathscr{S}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\!\to\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Recall the Levelt solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}
of quantum differential
equations (
6.23
).

Proposition 6.22
.

For any
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,
, the section
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-2.00002muf\mskip 0.99998mu

with values

(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-2.00002muf\mskip 0.99998mu)({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip-0.99998mu\bigr)\,f({\boldsymbol{z}})\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

belongs to
\,\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}
. Moreover, the map

(6.37)

\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu:\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu\to\mskip 2.00002mu\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu,\qquad f\mskip 2.00002mu\mapsto\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-2.00002muf\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

is an isomorphism.

Proof.

For any
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,
, the section
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-2.00002muf\,
solves
quantum differential equations (
6.23
). Since by formula (
6.28
),
Lemma
6.15
, and Theorem
5.22
, the section

\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
for

\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
, the section
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-2.00002muf\,
belongs to
\,\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.
Backwards, for any
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}
, the section
\,f^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}}
with values

(6.38)

f^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}}({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip-0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip-0.99998muf({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

does not depend on
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
because
\,f({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}})\,
is a solution of
quantum differential equations (
6.24
). Moreover,

\,\bigl(\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is holomorphic
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
since

\,\bigl(\det\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr)^{\mskip-2.00002mu-1}\mskip 0.99998mu
is entire
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. Thus
\,f^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}}\!\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
and

\,f=\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}f^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
. Hence, the map
\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,
is an isomorphism.
∎

For a solution
\,f\mskip-0.99998mu\in\mskip-0.99998mu\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
of quantum differential equations
(
6.23
), we call the section
\,f^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}}
defined by (
6.38
)
the
principal term
of
\,f\,
. For the solution

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}\,
corresponding to a Laurent polynomial

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
, see (
6.27
), the principal term is described
in Proposition
6.23
below.

Recall the functions
\,C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
and

,
see (

), (
