),

\displaystyle C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\mskip 2.00002mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\left(\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)\sum_{a=\smash{\lambda^{\mskip-2.00002mu(i-1)}}\mskip-0.99998mu+1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{a=1}^{\lambda^{(i)}}\,\prod_{b=\lambda^{(i)}+1}^{\lambda^{(i+1)}}\!\Gamma\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu-z_{b})/\kappa\bigr)\,,\kern-10.00002pt

and the function
\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
obtained from a Laurent
polynomial
\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
by substituting the variables
\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}\mskip 0.99998mu

and
\,\acute{z}\vphantom{z}_{a}\mskip 0.99998mu
with the exponentials
\,e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,\gamma_{i\mskip-0.99998mu,j}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}

and
\,e^{\mskip 0.99998mu2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}\,z_{a}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}
, respectively. Set

C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};\kappa)\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;,\kern 11.99998ptG^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};\kappa)\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)\,,\ignorespaces

and

\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{\acute{P}\vphantom{P}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}};{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

cf. (
6.6
).

Proposition 6.23
.

For a Laurent polynomial
\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\mskip 2.00002mu
, the values of the principal
term
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}
of the solution
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}
are

(6.39)

\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\bigl[\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\bigr]_{\boldsymbol{z}}\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Proof.

By Proposition
5.23
,

(6.40)

\Psi^{{\raise 0.3014pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varnothing$}}\mskip 0.99998mu}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{I\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\bigl\langle\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr\rangle\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\,.\kern-11.99998pt\ignorespaces

By Lemmas
6.6
,
6.8
, for any section
\,f\,
of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

[\mskip 0.99998muf(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 0.99998mu=\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{I\in\mskip 0.99998mu{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\bigl\langle f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr\rangle\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\,,\kern-11.99998pt

Hence, the right-hand sides of formulae (
6.39
) and (
6.40
)
coincide.
∎

The functions
\,C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
,

\,G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
, and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

are polynomials in
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}
analytically depending on
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. The sections
of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
defined by their cohomology classes can be thought of as
the cohomology classes of the respective analytic functions of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu
,

(6.41)

\displaystyle C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\mskip 2.00002mu\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\left(\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{j=i+1}^{N}\lambda_{j}\,-\,\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{j}\mskip-0.99998mu\right)\sum_{j=1}^{\smash{\lambda_{i}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\gamma_{i\mskip-0.99998mu,j}\mskip-0.99998mu/\mskip-0.99998mu\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-10.00002pt

\displaystyle G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\kappa^{\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{\{2\}}}\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\prod_{j=i+1}^{N}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{k=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,\prod_{l=1}^{\lambda_{j}}\,\Gamma\bigl(1+(\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muk}\mskip-0.99998mu-\gamma_{j,\mskip 2.00002mul})/\kappa\bigr)\,,\kern-10.00002pt

and
\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
. The class of
\;C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

is a product of exponentials of the equivariant first Chern classes

c_{1}(E_{i})\,=\,[\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma_{i,1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998mu\lambda_{i}}]\,
of
the vector bundles
\,E_{i}\mskip 2.00002mu
over
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
with fibers
\,F_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu/F_{i-1}\,
,
the class of
\;G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is the equivariant Gamma-class
of
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, and the class of
\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
is
the equivariant Chern character of the class of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
in
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

Define a map

(6.42)

{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998muK_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\mskip 2.00002mu\to\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

that sends the class
\,\mskip 0.99998mu[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
of the Laurent polynomial

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\,
in
\,K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
to the section of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu

with values
\,[\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\,
.
By Proposition
6.23
, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
sends
the class
\,[P\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
to the principal term of the solution

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}\mskip 0.99998mu
of the joint system of quantum differential
equations (
6.23
) and
qKZ
difference equations in cohomology
(

).

Theorem 6.24
.

Recall the space
\,{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\!\mskip-0.99998mu
of sections of
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu
holomorphic
in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
and not depending on
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
, and the space

\,\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
of solutions of quantum differential equations (

)
holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
.
Then the map
\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 0.99998mu:\mskip 0.99998muK_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\mskip 0.99998mu\to\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu

is well-defined and the following diagram is commutative,

(6.43)

K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})
\scriptstyle{\;{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\!\!}
\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle K}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\,\mskip 0.99998mu
\textstyle{\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{O}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\;\scriptstyle\mu^{\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}}_{H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
\textstyle{\!\mathscr{S}_{\!H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathcal{O}}$}}}}

Proof.

The statement follows from Proposition
5.24
by applying the stable
envelope map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
. In more detail, by the standard reasoning
the functions
\;C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
,

\;G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
,
\,\acute{P}\vphantom{P}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
, and

\,{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\bigl\langle\acute{P}\vphantom{P}^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,C^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,G^{\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,\ignorespaces\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr\rangle\,
are holomorphic in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,

for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\in L^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}
. Hence, the map
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathchoice{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\cyr B}}{\hbox{\scyr B}}{\hbox{\sscyr B}}}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
is well-defined. The
commutativity of diagram (
5.50
) follows from Proposition
6.23
.
∎

6.7.
The nonequivariant case
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}=0\,

In this section, we will discuss solutions of the quantum differential
equations for the cohomology algebra
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
of the partial flag
variety
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
by specializing the results obtained for the equivariant
case at
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}=0\,
.

The cohomology algebra
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
of the partial flag variety

\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
has the form

(6.44)

\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,(u-\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj})\,=\,u^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu\Bigr\rangle\,,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where
\,u\,
is a formal variable. It is isomorphic to the algebra

\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 2.00002mu
at
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-0.99998mu=0\,
, see (
6.3
).
We denote the class of a polynomial
\,f(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
by
\,[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\,
.

The integration map on
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
coincides with the map

\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\mskip 0.99998mu
at
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu=0\,
, see (
6.9
),

(6.45)

{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}:\mskip 0.99998mu\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\mskip 2.00002mu\to\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{C}}\,,\qquad[\mskip 0.99998muf\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu\mapsto\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\langle f\rangle\,,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

where

{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\langle f\rangle\,=\mskip 0.99998mu\biggl(\,\sum_{I\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{f({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}})}{R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{I}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\mskip 2.00002mu\biggr)\bigg|_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}=0}\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

see Lemmas
6.3
,
6.4
.

Consider the polynomials

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})=\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0)\,
and

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})=\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0)\,
.
By formulae (
6.12
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{0}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}),\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\,1\,,\kern-6.00006pt

\displaystyle\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu\sigma_{0}(I^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}),\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,\gamma_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj}^{\mskip 0.99998mun-\lambda^{(i)}}.\kern-6.00006pt

In general, the polynomials
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
,

\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
coincide with the

-type Schubert
polynomials, see (
6.13
),

(6.46)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\,\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma_{\mskip-2.00002mu\sigma_{\mskip-0.99998mu0}\mskip-0.99998mu(I)}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu)\,,\qquad\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,=\,\mathfrak{S}_{\sigma_{I}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu)\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

By Lemma
6.6
, the polynomials
\;\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
and

\;\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
are biorthogonal,

(6.47)

{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\langle\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip-0.99998muJ,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\rangle\,=\,\delta_{I\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998muJ}\,.\ignorespaces

Denote by
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 2.00002mu
the classes of

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. They are the fundamental classes of the Schubert
subvarieties in
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
defined for the opposite orderings of the chosen
basis of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}
in the standard way,

The quantum multiplication in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, is a deformation of
the multiplication in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
depending on quantum parameters

\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
. For
\mskip 2.00002muY\!\in\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, let
\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip-0.99998mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu
be
the operator of quantum multiplication by
\mskip 2.00002muY\mskip 0.99998mu
depending on
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
.

For
\,i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu
, denote
\,D_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu=\gamma_{i,1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
cf. (
6.2
). The quantum differential equations for

\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\mskip 0.99998mu
-valued functions of
\,{\boldsymbol{p}}\,
is the following
system of compatible differential equations,

(6.48)

\kappa\mskip 2.00002mup_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\frac{\partial f}{\partial\mskip 0.99998mup_{i}}\,=\,{[D_{i}]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 0.99998mu*_{\boldsymbol{p}}}\,f\,,\qquad i=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak N\mskip 2.00002mu,\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

where
\,\kappa\,
is a parameter of the equations. Equations (
6.48
)
coincide with the restriction of equivariant quantum differential equations
(
6.23
) to the subbundle
\,\displaystyle H^{*}\mskip-0.99998mu({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}\!\to\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{N}

of the bundle
\,H_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-2.00002mu\to\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\!\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}
located over the points

\,(\mskip 0.99998mu0\mskip 0.99998mu,{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu)\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\!\times{\mathbb{C}}^{N}
of the base.

Consider the
\,K\mskip-2.00002mu
-theory algebra
\,K({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
. Then

(6.49)

K({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{i=1}^{N}\prod_{j=1}^{\lambda_{i}}\,(u-\,\acute{\!\gamma}\vphantom{\gamma}_{i,\mskip 0.99998muj})\,=\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(u-1)\Bigr\rangle\,,\kern-16.00008pt\ignorespaces

where
\,u\,
is a formal variable. The evaluation map

{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\mskip 2.00002mu\to\mskip 2.00002mu{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 2.00002mu,\qquad P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}};\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}})\mapsto P\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}\mskip 0.99998mu;(1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak 1)\bigr)\,,\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

induces the isomorphism of

\,K({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\to K_{T}({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})/\langle\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}=(1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak 1)\mskip 0.99998mu\rangle\,
.
Denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998muP\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 2.00002mu1}\mskip 0.99998mu
the class of

\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}})\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
in
\,K({\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
.

Identify
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
with the subspace of

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 2.00002mu\acute{\mskip-2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{z}}}^{\pm 1}]\,
of Laurent polynomials
not depending on
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. For each
\,P\in{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}}^{\pm 1}]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
,
the solution
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}({\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

of equivariant quantum differential equations (
6.23
),
see Section
6.5
, is regular at
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}=0\,
, and the solution

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\Psi^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muP}(\mskip 0.99998mu0\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
of equations (
6.48
)
depends only on the class of
\,P\mskip 0.99998mu
in

. That is,
there is a well-defined map

where

).

Lemma 6.27
.

The function
\,F^{\scriptscriptstyle H}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu
is regular at
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}=0\mskip 2.00002mu
and

\,\mskip 0.99998mu[\mskip 0.99998muF\mskip 2.00002mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip-0.99998mu=[\mskip 0.99998muF^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\,
.

Proof.

By the standard facts on power series, it suffices to prove the statement
for a polynomial
\,F(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
. In the polynomial case,
formulae (
6.53
), (
6.4
) yield
\,F(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})=F^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,
.
Then
\,\mskip 0.99998mu[\mskip 0.99998muF(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip-0.99998mu=[\mskip 0.99998muF^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip-0.99998mu=[\mskip 0.99998muF^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\,
,
because
\,F^{\scriptscriptstyle H}\mskip-2.00002mu(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu)\,
is symmetric in
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
.
∎

Recall the functions
\,C^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\,G^{\mskip 0.99998mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,

\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
, and the class of

of the class of the Laurent polynomial
\,P(\mskip 0.99998mu\acute{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\vphantom{{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}})\,
in

-function

(6.57)

{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\bigl\langle\mskip-0.99998mu\bigl(\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\widehat{\Psi}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}(\mskip 0.99998mu0\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muf\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu0}\bigr\rangle\mskip 2.00002mu=\,{\mathcal{E}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\bigl\langle\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 2.00002muf\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr\rangle\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Proposition 6.30
.

For any
\,I\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,

(6.59)

\kern-5.0pt\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI}\mskip-2.00002mu({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\,\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\!\mskip-2.00002mu\sum_{{\boldsymbol{m}}\mskip 0.99998mu\in\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{N-1}\!}\![\mskip 0.99998mu\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\,\bigl(\mskip-0.99998mu(-\mskip 0.99998mu\kappa)^{-\lambda_{i}-\lambda_{i+1}}\mskip 2.00002mup_{i+1}/p_{i}\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr)^{\mskip 0.99998mum_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu\prod_{i=1}^{N}\,p_{i}^{\mskip 0.99998mu[D_{i}]_{0}/\kappa}\mskip 0.99998mu,\kern-10.00002pt

where the functions
\,\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}\mskip-0.99998mu
are given by (
6.30
),

\;\Omega^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-2.00002mu=e^{\mskip 0.99998mu\sum_{\mskip 0.99998mui<j}^{{\raise 0.43054pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}p_{j}\mskip-0.99998mu/(\kappa\mskip 0.99998mup_{i})}

in which the sum is taken over all pairs
\,i<j\,

\,\lambda_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu=1\,

and
\,\lambda_{i+1}\mskip-0.99998mu=\allowbreak\dots=\allowbreak\lambda_{j}\mskip-0.99998mu=0\,
,
see (

),
and
\,D_{i}\mskip-0.99998mu=\gamma_{i,1}\mskip-0.99998mu+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\gamma_{i,\mskip 2.00002mu\lambda_{i}}\mskip 2.00002mu
.

Proof.

By Proposition
6.19
, the functions

\,\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
are regular at

.
Thus the classes
\;[\mskip 0.99998mu\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;0\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\mskip 0.99998mu]_{\mskip 0.99998mu0}\mskip 2.00002mu

in formula (
6.59
) are well defined. Then the statement follows from
Proposition
6.21
and formulae (
6.57
), (
6.33
),
(
6.29
).
∎

By inspection, the function
\,\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-0.99998mu({\boldsymbol{p}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
in this paper
coincides with the nonequivariant
\mskip 0.99998muJ
-function of
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip 2.00002mu
obtained
in
[
BCK
]
up to a change of notation.

In more detail, formula (
6.30
) for the function

\,\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu\Gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,
takes the form

(6.60)

\hat{\mathcal{J}}{}^{\mskip-0.99998mu\scriptscriptstyle H}_{\!\mskip-2.00002muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}\mskip-0.99998mu,\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{m}}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,=\mskip 2.00002mu\sum_{i=1}^{N-1}\!\sum_{\genfrac{}{}{0.0pt}{1}{\scriptstyle\lb\in\:\smash{\Z^{\:\la\+1}}\!\vp{|^{1^1}}}{\scriptstyle\!|\:\lb^{(i)}\<|=\:m_i}}\mskip-0.99998mu\sum_{J\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}}\frac{A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}\mskip-0.99998mu(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-2.00002mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip 0.99998mu;\kappa)\,V_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}(\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}{\hbox{$A^{\mskip-2.00002mu{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}}$}\mskip-0.99998mu(\Sigma_{J};{\boldsymbol{z}})\,R_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}({\boldsymbol{z}}_{\sigma_{J}}\mskip-0.99998mu)}\;,\kern-10.00002pt

because
\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\mskip-2.00002muI^{\mskip 0.99998mu\min}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(\Sigma_{J}\mskip-2.00002mu-{\boldsymbol{l}}\kappa\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})=1\,
, see Lemma

.
In (

\,{\boldsymbol{m}}=(m_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak m_{N-1})\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geqslant 0}^{N\mskip-0.99998mu-1}

\;|\mskip 2.00002mu{\boldsymbol{l}}^{(i)}|=\mskip 0.99998mul^{\mskip 0.99998mu(i)}_{j}\!+\allowbreak\dots+\allowbreak\mskip 0.99998mul^{\mskip 0.99998mu(i)}_{\lambda^{(i)}}\,
,

agrees term by term with the expression for the class

6.9.
Example
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,
,
\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}={\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}

Throughout this section,
let
\,N=2\,
,
\,n\geqslant 2\,
,
\,{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}=(1,n-1)\,
,

\,{\mathcal{F}}_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}={\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}
.

Denote
\,\gamma=\gamma_{1,1}\mskip 2.00002mu
. Identify
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu]\,
with the subspace
of
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}
consisting of polynomials not depending
on
\,\gamma_{\mskip 0.99998mu2,1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak\mskip-0.99998mu\gamma_{\mskip 0.99998mu2,\mskip 0.99998mun-1}\mskip 2.00002mu
, and
\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}

with the subspace of
{\,\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}\mskip 0.99998mu]^{\mskip 0.99998muS_{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip-2.00002mu\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\,}
consisting
of polynomials not depending on
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\,
. Then the equivariant cohomology
algebra
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,
, see (
6.1
), can be presented
as follows

(6.64)

\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu]\otimes{\mathbb{C}}[{\boldsymbol{z}}]\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(\gamma-z_{a})\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu0\mskip 0.99998mu\Bigr\rangle\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Similarly, for any
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
, the algebra

\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,
can be presented as follows

(6.65)

\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\,=\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu]\mskip 2.00002mu\Big/\Bigl\langle\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(\gamma-z^{0}_{a})\mskip 2.00002mu=\mskip 2.00002mu0\mskip 0.99998mu\Bigr\rangle\,.\kern-20.00003pt

For any polynomial
\,f(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, the polynomial
\,{\mathcal{E}}\langle f\rangle({\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
see (
6.7
), can be evaluated as an integral

(6.66)

{\mathcal{E}}\langle f\rangle({\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\frac{1}{2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}}\,\int\limits_{\!\!C\,}\frac{f(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})}{(\gamma-z_{1})\dots(\gamma-z_{n})}\;d\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

over a contour
\,\mskip 0.99998muC\mskip 2.00002mu
encircling
\,z_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z_{n}\mskip 2.00002mu
counterclockwise.
Similarly, for any polynomial
\,f(\gamma)\,
and
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}\mskip-2.00002mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
,

(6.67)

{\mathcal{E}}_{{\boldsymbol{z}}^{0}}\langle f\rangle\,=\,\frac{1}{2\mskip 0.99998mu\pi\mskip 0.99998mu\sqrt{\mskip-0.99998mu-1}}\,\int\limits_{\!\!C\,}\frac{f(\gamma)}{(\gamma-z^{0}_{1})\dots(\gamma-z^{0}_{n})}\;d\mskip 0.99998mu\gamma\kern-10.00002pt

for a contour
\,\mskip 0.99998muC\mskip 2.00002mu
encircling
\,z^{0}_{1},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak z^{0}_{n}\mskip 2.00002mu
counterclockwise.

Following Section
5.7
, denote by
\,[\mskip 0.99998mua\mskip 0.99998mu]
the element

\bigl(\{a\},\{\mskip 0.99998mu1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak a-1,a+1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\mskip 0.99998mu\}\bigr)\mskip-0.99998mu\in{{\mathcal{I}}_{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}}
. The polynomials

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
are respectively
obtained from the functions
\mskip 2.00002mu{\check{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,

\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
, see (
5.57
), (
5.55
),
by the substitution
\,\mskip 0.99998mut=\gamma\,
,

(6.68)

\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\prod_{c=a+1}^{n}\mskip-2.00002mu(\gamma-z_{b})\,,\kern 13.00005pt\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\prod_{c=1}^{a-1}\,(\gamma-z_{b})\,,\kern 16.99998pta=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n\,.\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

The biorthogonality relation, see Lemma
6.6
,

(6.69)

{\mathcal{E}}\langle\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[b]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\rangle\,=\,\delta_{a,\mskip 0.99998mub}\kern-10.00002pt\ignorespaces

is clear from formula (

).

Consider a polynomial

(6.70)

\,\,\smash{\overline{\!\!W\!\mskip-2.00002mu}}\;\vphantom{W}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\frac{1}{t-\gamma}\,\biggl(\,\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(t-z_{a})\mskip 0.99998mu-\mskip 0.99998mu\prod_{a=1}^{n}\,(\gamma-z_{a})\mskip-0.99998mu\biggr)\mskip 2.00002mu,

and its image
\,\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu\,\,\smash{\overline{\!\!W\!\mskip-2.00002mu}}\;\vphantom{W}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,

in
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mut\mskip 0.99998mu]\otimes\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1};{\mathbb{C}})\,
.
Recall the polynomial
\,{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\,

),
and its image
\,\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,

in
\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mut\mskip 0.99998mu]\otimes\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1};{\mathbb{C}})\,
.
By relations (
6.1
), (
6.64
),

\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu{\mskip 0.99998mu\widehat{\mskip-0.99998muW\mskip-0.99998mu}\mskip 0.99998mu}{\vphantom{W}}^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,=\,\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu\,\,\smash{\overline{\!\!W\!\mskip-2.00002mu}}\;\vphantom{W}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

Thus Theorem
6.9
for
\,{\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}
reads as follows.

(6.71)

\bigl[\mskip 0.99998mu\,\,\smash{\overline{\!\!W\!\mskip-2.00002mu}}\;\vphantom{W}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\mskip 0.99998mu\bigr]\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\bigl[\mskip 2.00002mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\bigr]\,.\kern-20.00003pt\ignorespaces

In fact, formula (
6.71
) is the image

\,{\mathbb{C}}[\mskip 0.99998mut\mskip 0.99998mu]\otimes\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1};{\mathbb{C}})\,
of the equality of polynomials

(6.72)

\,\,\smash{\overline{\!\!W\!\mskip-2.00002mu}}\;\vphantom{W}_{\!\mskip-0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,=\,\sum_{a=1}^{n}\,W^{\raise 0.60275pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\circ$}}_{[a]}(t\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,.\kern-20.00003pt

Recall that for
\,{\boldsymbol{z}}\mskip-0.99998mu\in{\mathbb{C}}^{n}\mskip 0.99998mu
and
\,a=1,\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak n
, the classes of the
polynomials
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,
,
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]}(\gamma\mskip 0.99998mu;{\boldsymbol{z}})\,

in
\,\displaystyle H^{*}_{T}({\mathbb{C}}\mskip 0.99998mu{\mathbb{P}}^{\mskip 2.00002mun-1}\mskip-0.99998mu;{\mathbb{C}})_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{z}}}\,
are denoted by

\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]\mskip 0.99998mu,\,{\boldsymbol{z}}}\mskip 2.00002mu
,
\,\mskip 0.99998mu\mathrm{Stab}^{\scriptscriptstyle\mskip-0.99998mu\mathrm{o\mskip-0.99998mup}}_{\mskip 0.99998mu[a]\mskip 0.99998mu,\,{\boldsymbol{z}}}
.
Consider a basis
\,v_{[\mskip 0.99998mu1\mskip 0.99998mu]},\allowbreak\dots,\allowbreak v_{[\mskip 0.99998mun\mskip 0.99998mu]}\mskip 0.99998mu

\,({\mathbb{C}}^{\mskip 0.99998mu2})^{\otimes\mskip 0.99998mun}_{\mskip 0.99998mu{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}}\mskip 0.99998mu
